export function createQueryResult<T extends QueryParameter[]>(
    world: World,
    entities: Entity[],
    query: QueryInstance,
    params: QueryParameter[]
): QueryResult<T> {
    const traits: Trait[] = [];
    const stores: Store<any>[] = [];

    getQueryStores(params, traits, stores, world);

    const results = Object.assign(entities, {
        readEach(
            callback: (state: InstancesFromParameters<T>, entity: Entity, index: number) => void
        ) {
            const state = Array.from({ length: traits.length }) as InstancesFromParameters<T>;

            for (let i = 0; i < entities.length; i++) {
                const entity = entities[i];
                const eid = getEntityId(entity);

                // Create snapshots without atomic tracking
                createSnapshots(eid, traits, stores, state);

                callback(state, entity, i);
            }

            return results;
        },

        updateEach(
            callback: (state: InstancesFromParameters<T>, entity: Entity, index: number) => void,
            options: QueryResultOptions = { changeDetection: 'auto' }
        ) {
            const state = Array.from({ length: traits.length });

            // Inline all three permutations of updateEach for performance.
            if (options.changeDetection === 'auto') {
                const changedPairs: [Entity, Trait][] = [];
                const atomicSnapshots: any[] = [];
                const trackedIndices: number[] = [];
                const untrackedIndices: number[] = [];

                getTrackedTraits(traits, world, query, trackedIndices, untrackedIndices);

                for (let i = 0; i < entities.length; i++) {
                    const entity = entities[i];
                    const eid = getEntityId(entity);

                    createSnapshotsWithAtomic(eid, traits, stores, state, atomicSnapshots);
                    callback(state as unknown as InstancesFromParameters<T>, entity, i);

                    // Skip if the entity has been destroyed.
                    if (!world.has(entity)) continue;

                    // Commit all changes back to the stores for tracked traits.
                    for (let j = 0; j < trackedIndices.length; j++) {
                        const index = trackedIndices[j];
                        const trait = traits[index];
                        const ctx = trait[$internal];
                        const newValue = state[index];
                        const store = stores[index];

                        let changed = false;
                        if (ctx.type === 'aos') {
                            changed = ctx.fastSetWithChangeDetection(eid, store, newValue);
                            if (!changed) {
                                changed = !shallowEqual(newValue, atomicSnapshots[index]);
                            }
                        } else {
                            changed = ctx.fastSetWithChangeDetection(eid, store, newValue);
                        }

                        // Collect changed traits.
                        if (changed) changedPairs.push([entity, trait] as const);
                    }

                    // Commit all changes back to the stores for untracked traits.
                    for (let j = 0; j < untrackedIndices.length; j++) {
                        const index = untrackedIndices[j];
                        const trait = traits[index];
                        const ctx = trait[$internal];
                        const store = stores[index];
                        ctx.fastSet(eid, store, state[index]);
                    }
                }

                // Trigger change events for each entity that was modified.
                for (let i = 0; i < changedPairs.length; i++) {
                    const [entity, trait] = changedPairs[i];
                    setChanged(world, entity, trait);
                }
            } else if (options.changeDetection === 'always') {
                const changedPairs: [Entity, Trait][] = [];
                const atomicSnapshots: any[] = [];

                for (let i = 0; i < entities.length; i++) {
                    const entity = entities[i];
                    const eid = getEntityId(entity);

                    createSnapshotsWithAtomic(eid, traits, stores, state, atomicSnapshots);
                    callback(state as unknown as InstancesFromParameters<T>, entity, i);

                    // Skip if the entity has been destroyed.
                    if (!world.has(entity)) continue;

                    // Commit all changes back to the stores.
                    for (let j = 0; j < traits.length; j++) {
                        const trait = traits[j];
                        const ctx = trait[$internal];
                        const newValue = state[j];

                        let changed = false;
                        if (ctx.type === 'aos') {
                            changed = ctx.fastSetWithChangeDetection(eid, stores[j], newValue);
                            if (!changed) {
                                changed = !shallowEqual(newValue, atomicSnapshots[j]);
                            }
                        } else {
                            changed = ctx.fastSetWithChangeDetection(eid, stores[j], newValue);
                        }

                        // Collect changed traits.
                        if (changed) changedPairs.push([entity, trait] as const);
                    }
                }

                // Trigger change events for each entity that was modified.
                for (let i = 0; i < changedPairs.length; i++) {
                    const [entity, trait] = changedPairs[i];
                    setChanged(world, entity, trait);
                }
            } else if (options.changeDetection === 'never') {
                for (let i = 0; i < entities.length; i++) {
                    const entity = entities[i];
                    const eid = getEntityId(entity);
                    createSnapshots(eid, traits, stores, state);
                    callback(state as unknown as InstancesFromParameters<T>, entity, i);

                    // Skip if the entity has been destroyed.
                    if (!world.has(entity)) continue;

                    // Commit all changes back to the stores.
                    for (let j = 0; j < traits.length; j++) {
                        const trait = traits[j];
                        const ctx = trait[$internal];
                        ctx.fastSet(eid, stores[j], state[j]);
                    }
                }
            }

            return results;
        },

        useStores(callback: (stores: StoresFromParameters<T>, entities: readonly Entity[]) => void) {
            callback(stores as unknown as StoresFromParameters<T>, entities);
            return results;
        },

        select<U extends QueryParameter[]>(...params: U): QueryResult<U> {
            traits.length = 0;
            stores.length = 0;
            getQueryStores(params, traits, stores, world);
            return results as unknown as QueryResult<U>;
        },

        sort(
            callback: (a: Entity, b: Entity) => number = (a, b) => getEntityId(a) - getEntityId(b)
        ): QueryResult<T> {
            Array.prototype.sort.call(entities, callback);
            return results;
        },
    });

    return results;
}

import { $internal } from '../common';
import type { Entity } from '../entity/types';
import { getEntityId } from '../entity/utils/pack-entity';
import { getRelationData, setRelationData } from '../relation/relation';
import type { Relation, RelationPair } from '../relation/types';
import { isRelationPair } from '../relation/utils/is-relation';
import { Store } from '../storage';
import { getStore } from '../trait/trait';
import type { Trait } from '../trait/types';
import { shallowEqual } from '../utils/shallow-equal';
import type { World } from '../world';
import { isModifier } from './modifier';
import { setChanged, setPairChanged } from './modifiers/changed';
import type {
    InstancesFromParameters,
    QueryInstance,
    QueryParameter,
    QueryResult,
    QueryResultOptions,
    StoresFromParameters,
} from './types';

export function createQueryResult<T extends QueryParameter[]>(
    world: World,
    entities: Entity[],
    query: QueryInstance,
    params: QueryParameter[]
): QueryResult<T> {
    const traits: Trait[] = [];
    const stores: Store<any>[] = [];
    const relationPairs: (RelationPair | undefined)[] = [];

    getQueryStores(params, traits, stores, relationPairs, world);

    const results = Object.assign(entities, {
        readEach(
            callback: (state: InstancesFromParameters<T>, entity: Entity, index: number) => void
        ) {
            const state = Array.from({ length: traits.length }) as InstancesFromParameters<T>;

            for (let i = 0; i < entities.length; i++) {
                const entity = entities[i];

                // Create snapshots without atomic tracking
                createSnapshots(world, entity, traits, stores, relationPairs, state);

                callback(state, entity, i);
            }

            return results;
        },

        updateEach(
            callback: (state: InstancesFromParameters<T>, entity: Entity, index: number) => void,
            options: QueryResultOptions = { changeDetection: 'auto' }
        ) {
            const state = Array.from({ length: traits.length });

            // Inline all three permutations of updateEach for performance.
            if (options.changeDetection === 'auto') {
                const changedPairs: [Entity, Trait, Entity?][] = [];
                const atomicSnapshots: any[] = [];
                const trackedIndices: number[] = [];
                const untrackedIndices: number[] = [];

                getTrackedTraits(traits, world, query, trackedIndices, untrackedIndices);

                for (let i = 0; i < entities.length; i++) {
                    const entity = entities[i];
                    const eid = getEntityId(entity);

                    createSnapshotsWithAtomic(world, entity, traits, stores, relationPairs, state, atomicSnapshots);
                    callback(state as unknown as InstancesFromParameters<T>, entity, i);

                    // Skip if the entity has been destroyed.
                    if (!world.has(entity)) continue;

                    // Commit all changes back to the stores for tracked traits.
                    for (let j = 0; j < trackedIndices.length; j++) {
                        const index = trackedIndices[j];
                        const trait = traits[index];
                        const newValue = state[index];
                        const oldValue = atomicSnapshots[index];

                        const changed = commitPairOrTrait(
                            world,
                            entity,
                            eid,
                            trait,
                            stores[index],
                            relationPairs[index],
                            newValue,
                            oldValue
                        );

                        // Collect changed traits.
                        if (changed) {
                            const pair = relationPairs[index];
                            changedPairs.push([
                                entity,
                                trait,
                                pair && typeof pair[$internal].target === 'number'
                                    ? pair[$internal].target
                                    : undefined,
                            ]);
                        }
                    }

                    // Commit all changes back to the stores for untracked traits.
                    for (let j = 0; j < untrackedIndices.length; j++) {
                        const index = untrackedIndices[j];
                        const trait = traits[index];
                        const newValue = state[index];

                        commitPairOrTrait(
                            world,
                            entity,
                            eid,
                            trait,
                            stores[index],
                            relationPairs[index],
                            newValue,
                            undefined
                        );
                    }
                }

                // Trigger change events for each entity that was modified.
                for (let i = 0; i < changedPairs.length; i++) {
                    const [entity, trait, target] = changedPairs[i];
                    if (target !== undefined) {
                        setPairChanged(world, entity, trait, target);
                    } else {
                        setChanged(world, entity, trait);
                    }
                }
            } else if (options.changeDetection === 'always') {
                const changedPairs: [Entity, Trait, Entity?][] = [];
                const atomicSnapshots: any[] = [];

                for (let i = 0; i < entities.length; i++) {
                    const entity = entities[i];
                    const eid = getEntityId(entity);

                    createSnapshotsWithAtomic(world, entity, traits, stores, relationPairs, state, atomicSnapshots);
                    callback(state as unknown as InstancesFromParameters<T>, entity, i);

                    // Skip if the entity has been destroyed.
                    if (!world.has(entity)) continue;

                    // Commit all changes back to the stores.
                    for (let j = 0; j < traits.length; j++) {
                        const trait = traits[j];
                        const newValue = state[j];

                        const changed = commitPairOrTrait(
                            world,
                            entity,
                            eid,
                            trait,
                            stores[j],
                            relationPairs[j],
                            newValue,
                            atomicSnapshots[j]
                        );

                        // Collect changed traits.
                        if (changed) {
                            const pair = relationPairs[j];
                            changedPairs.push([
                                entity,
                                trait,
                                pair && typeof pair[$internal].target === 'number'
                                    ? pair[$internal].target
                                    : undefined,
                            ]);
                        }
                    }
                }

                // Trigger change events for each entity that was modified.
                for (let i = 0; i < changedPairs.length; i++) {
                    const [entity, trait, target] = changedPairs[i];
                    if (target !== undefined) {
                        setPairChanged(world, entity, trait, target);
                    } else {
                        setChanged(world, entity, trait);
                    }
                }
            } else if (options.changeDetection === 'never') {
                for (let i = 0; i < entities.length; i++) {
                    const entity = entities[i];
                    const eid = getEntityId(entity);
                    createSnapshots(world, entity, traits, stores, relationPairs, state);
                    callback(state as unknown as InstancesFromParameters<T>, entity, i);

                    // Skip if the entity has been destroyed.
                    if (!world.has(entity)) continue;

                    // Commit all changes back to the stores.
                    for (let j = 0; j < traits.length; j++) {
                        const trait = traits[j];
                        commitPairOrTrait(
                            world,
                            entity,
                            eid,
                            trait,
                            stores[j],
                            relationPairs[j],
                            state[j],
                            undefined
                        );
                    }
                }
            }

            return results;
        },

        useStores(callback: (stores: StoresFromParameters<T>, entities: readonly Entity[]) => void) {
            callback(stores as unknown as StoresFromParameters<T>, entities);
            return results;
        },

        select<U extends QueryParameter[]>(...params: U): QueryResult<U> {
            traits.length = 0;
            stores.length = 0;
            relationPairs.length = 0;
            getQueryStores(params, traits, stores, relationPairs, world);
            return results as unknown as QueryResult<U>;
        },

        sort(
            callback: (a: Entity, b: Entity) => number = (a, b) => getEntityId(a) - getEntityId(b)
        ): QueryResult<T> {
            Array.prototype.sort.call(entities, callback);
            return results;
        },
    });

    return results;
}

/* @inline */ function getTrackedTraits(
    traits: Trait[],
    world: World,
    query: QueryInstance,
    trackedIndices: number[],
    untrackedIndices: number[]
) {
    for (let i = 0; i < traits.length; i++) {
        const trait = traits[i];
        const hasTracked = world[$internal].trackedTraits.has(trait);
        const hasChanged = query.hasChangedModifiers && query.changedTraits.has(trait);

        if (hasTracked || hasChanged) trackedIndices.push(i);
        else untrackedIndices.push(i);
    }
}

/* @inline */ function createSnapshots(
    world: World,
    entity: Entity,
    traits: Trait[],
    stores: Store<any>[],
    relationPairs: (RelationPair | undefined)[],
    state: any[]
) {
    for (let i = 0; i < traits.length; i++) {
        const trait = traits[i];
        const pair = relationPairs[i];
        const target = pair && typeof pair[$internal].target === 'number' ? pair[$internal].target : undefined;

        if (target !== undefined) {
            const relation = pair![$internal].relation as Relation<Trait>;
            state[i] = getRelationData(world, entity, relation, target);
        } else {
            const ctx = trait[$internal];
            state[i] = ctx.get(getEntityId(entity), stores[i]);
        }
    }
}

/* @inline */ function createSnapshotsWithAtomic(
    world: World,
    entity: Entity,
    traits: Trait[],
    stores: Store<any>[],
    relationPairs: (RelationPair | undefined)[],
    state: any[],
    atomicSnapshots: any[]
) {
    for (let j = 0; j < traits.length; j++) {
        const trait = traits[j];
        const pair = relationPairs[j];
        const target = pair && typeof pair[$internal].target === 'number' ? pair[$internal].target : undefined;

        if (target !== undefined) {
            const relation = pair![$internal].relation as Relation<Trait>;
            const value = getRelationData(world, entity, relation, target);
            state[j] = value;
            atomicSnapshots[j] = trait[$internal].type === 'aos' ? { ...value } : null;
        } else {
            const ctx = trait[$internal];
            const value = ctx.get(getEntityId(entity), stores[j]);
            state[j] = value;
            atomicSnapshots[j] = ctx.type === 'aos' ? { ...value } : null;
        }
    }
}

/* @inline */ function commitPairOrTrait(
    world: World,
    entity: Entity,
    eid: number,
    trait: Trait,
    store: Store<any>,
    pair: RelationPair | undefined,
    newValue: any,
    oldValue: any
): boolean {
    const target = pair && typeof pair[$internal].target === 'number' ? pair[$internal].target : undefined;

    if (target !== undefined) {
        const relation = pair![$internal].relation as Relation<Trait>;
        const previous = getRelationData(world, entity, relation, target);
        const changed = !shallowEqual(previous, newValue);
        if (changed) setRelationData(world, entity, relation, target, newValue);
        return changed;
    }

    const ctx = trait[$internal];
    if (ctx.type === 'aos') {
        const changed = ctx.fastSetWithChangeDetection(eid, store, newValue);
        if (!changed) {
            return !shallowEqual(newValue, oldValue);
        }
        return changed;
    }
    return ctx.fastSetWithChangeDetection(eid, store, newValue);
}

/* @inline */ export function getQueryStores<T extends QueryParameter[]>(
    params: T,
    traits: Trait[],
    stores: Store<any>[],
    relationPairs: (RelationPair | undefined)[],
    world: World
) {
    for (let i = 0; i < params.length; i++) {
        const param = params[i];

        // Handle relation pairs
        if (isRelationPair(param)) {
            const pairCtx = param[$internal];
            const relation = pairCtx.relation as Relation<Trait>;
            const baseTrait = relation[$internal].trait;
            if (baseTrait[$internal].type !== 'tag') {
                traits.push(baseTrait);
                stores.push(getStore(world, baseTrait));
                relationPairs.push(typeof pairCtx.target === 'number' ? param : undefined);
            }
            continue;
        }

        if (isModifier(param)) {
            // Skip not modifier.
            if (param.type === 'not') continue;

            const pairTraits = new Set<number>();
            if (param.pairs) {
                for (const pair of param.pairs) {
                    const pairCtx = pair[$internal];
                    const relation = pairCtx.relation as Relation<Trait>;
                    const baseTrait = relation[$internal].trait;
                    pairTraits.add(baseTrait.id);
                    if (baseTrait[$internal].type === 'tag') continue;

                    traits.push(baseTrait);
                    stores.push(getStore(world, baseTrait));
                    relationPairs.push(typeof pairCtx.target === 'number' ? pair : undefined);
                }
            }

            const modifierTraits = param.traits;
            for (const trait of modifierTraits) {
                if (pairTraits.has(trait.id)) continue; // Already included via pair
                if (trait[$internal].type === 'tag') continue; // Skip tags
                traits.push(trait);
                stores.push(getStore(world, trait));
                relationPairs.push(undefined);
            }
        } else {
            const trait = param as Trait;
            if (trait[$internal].type === 'tag') continue; // Skip tags
            traits.push(trait);
            stores.push(getStore(world, trait));
            relationPairs.push(undefined);
        }
    }
}

export function createEmptyQueryResult(): QueryResult<QueryParameter[]> {
    const results = Object.assign([], {
        readEach: () => results,
        updateEach: () => results,
        useStores: () => results,
        select: () => results,
        sort: () => results,
    }) as QueryResult<QueryParameter[]>;

    return results;
}

// Cached no-op result methods for relation-only queries
const relationOnlyMethods = {
    readEach(this: QueryResult<any>, callback: any) {
        // No traits to read, just iterate entities
        for (let i = 0; i < this.length; i++) {
            callback([], this[i], i);
        }
        return this;
    },
    updateEach(this: QueryResult<any>, callback: any) {
        // No traits to update, just iterate entities
        for (let i = 0; i < this.length; i++) {
            callback([], this[i], i);
        }
        return this;
    },
    useStores(this: QueryResult<any>, callback: any) {
        // No stores, call with empty array
        callback([], this);
        return this;
    },
    select(this: QueryResult<any>) {
        // No-op, nothing to select
        return this;
    },
};

/**
 * Lightweight query result for relation-only queries.
 * Skips store/trait setup since we only need to iterate entities.
 */
export function createRelationOnlyQueryResult<T extends QueryParameter[]>(
    entities: Entity[]
): QueryResult<T> {
    const results = Object.assign(entities, {
        readEach: relationOnlyMethods.readEach,
        updateEach: relationOnlyMethods.updateEach,
        useStores: relationOnlyMethods.useStores,
        select: relationOnlyMethods.select,
        sort(
            callback: (a: Entity, b: Entity) => number = (a, b) => getEntityId(a) - getEntityId(b)
        ): QueryResult<T> {
            Array.prototype.sort.call(entities, callback);
            return results;
        },
    }) as unknown as QueryResult<T>;

    return results;
}

        child2.remove(ChildOf(parent));
        child2.add(ChildOf(parent));""", """        // Reverse: remove then add also cancels (factory after initial pair exists)

        const Added2 = createAdded();
        const Removed2 = createRemoved();

        child2.remove(ChildOf(parent));
        child2.add(ChildOf(parent));""")
# Destruction: create Removed after entity exists
s=s.replace("""    it('entity destruction fires pair-level removal for all active pairs', () => {
        const Removed = createRemoved();
        const Likes = relation();

        const target = world.spawn();
        const entity = world.spawn(Likes(target));

        entity.destroy();""", """    it('entity destruction fires pair-level removal for all active pairs', () => {
        const Likes = relation();

        const target = world.spawn();
        const entity = world.spawn(Likes(target));

        // Factory created after the pair exists so destruction is a real removal.
        const Removed = createRemoved();

        entity.destroy();""")
open(p,'w').write(s)

STEP 83 CALL 1 RETURN 0
COMMAND:
cd /app && cat >> packages/core/tests/pair-tracking-modifiers.test.ts <<'EOF'

describe('pair tracking modifiers incremental paths', () => {
    const world = createWorld();
    world.init();

    beforeEach(() => {
        world.reset();
    });

    it('Removed pair works when the query is created before the removal', () => {
        const Likes = relation();
        const apple = world.spawn();
        const banana = world.spawn();
        const player = world.spawn(Likes(apple), Likes(banana));
        const Removed = createRemoved();

        expect(world.query(Removed(Likes(apple)))).toHaveLength(0);

        player.remove(Likes(apple));

        expect(world.query(Removed(Likes(apple)))).toContain(player);
    });

    it('Changed pair works when the query is created before the change', () => {
        const Likes = relation({ store: { amount: 0 } });
        const parent = world.spawn();
        const child = world.spawn(Likes(parent));
        const Changed = createChanged();

        expect(world.query(Changed(Likes(parent)))).toHaveLength(0);

        child.set(Likes(parent), { amount: 2 });

        expect(world.query(Changed(Likes(parent)))).toContain(child);
    });

    it('exclusive replacement works when queries are created before replacement', () => {
        const Targeting = relation({ exclusive: true });
        const oldTarget = world.spawn();
        const newTarget = world.spawn();
        const goblin = world.spawn(Targeting(oldTarget));
        const Added = createAdded();
        const Removed = createRemoved();

        expect(world.query(Added(Targeting(newTarget)))).toHaveLength(0);
        expect(world.query(Removed(Targeting(oldTarget)))).toHaveLength(0);

        goblin.add(Targeting(newTarget));

        expect(world.query(Added(Targeting(newTarget)))).toContain(goblin);
        expect(world.query(Removed(Targeting(oldTarget)))).toContain(goblin);
    });

    it('cancellation works when queries are created before opposite events', () => {
        const Added = createAdded();
        const Removed = createRemoved();
        const ChildOf = relation();
        const parent = world.spawn();
        const child = world.spawn();

        expect(world.query(Added(ChildOf(parent)))).toHaveLength(0);
        expect(world.query(Removed(ChildOf(parent)))).toHaveLength(0);

        child.add(ChildOf(parent));
        child.remove(ChildOf(parent));

        expect(world.query(Added(ChildOf(parent)))).toHaveLength(0);
        expect(world.query(Removed(ChildOf(parent)))).toHaveLength(0);
    });

    it('destruction works when query is created before destruction', () => {
        const Likes = relation();
        const target = world.spawn();
        const entity = world.spawn(Likes(target));
        const Removed = createRemoved();

        expect(world.query(Removed(Likes(target)))).toHaveLength(0);

        entity.destroy();

        expect(world.query(Removed(Likes(target)))).toContain(entity);
    });
});
EOF

old3="""export function updateQueriesForRelationPairChange(
    world: World,
    relationTrait: Trait,
    entity: Entity,
    target: Entity,
    eventType: 'add' | 'remove' | 'change'
) {
    const ctx = world[$internal];
    const instance = getTraitInstance(ctx.traitInstances, relationTrait);
    if (!instance) return;

    const { generationId, bitflag } = instance;

    for (const query of instance.trackingQueries) {
        const match = query.checkTracking(world, entity, eventType, generationId, bitflag, target);
        if (match) query.add(entity);
        else query.remove(world, entity);
    }
}"""
new3="""export function updateQueriesForRelationPairChange(
    world: World,
    relationTrait: Trait,
    entity: Entity,
    target: Entity,
    eventType: 'add' | 'remove' | 'change',
    canceled = false
) {
    const ctx = world[$internal];
    const instance = getTraitInstance(ctx.traitInstances, relationTrait);
    if (!instance) return;

    const { generationId, bitflag } = instance;

    for (const query of instance.trackingQueries) {
        const match = query.checkTracking(
            world,
            entity,
            eventType,
            generationId,
            bitflag,
            target,
            canceled
        );
        if (match) query.add(entity);
        else query.remove(world, entity);
    }
}"""

old2="""        if (affected) {
            // Cross-event invalidation:
            // - Remove event invalidates Added/Changed tracking
            // - Add event invalidates Removed/Changed tracking
            if (eventType === 'remove') {

check-query-tracking updated

        // Check required traits
        if (required && (entityMask & required) !== required) return false;

        // Check Or traits
        if (or !== 0 && (entityMask & or) === 0) return false;
    }

    // 2. Process tracking groups - update trackers and check cross-event invalidation
    // Also track OR group state to avoid second loop when possible

    let anyOrMatched = false;

    for (let i = 0; i < trackingGroupsLen; i++) {
        const group = trackingGroups[i];
        const groupType = group.type;
        const groupLogic = group.logic;
        const groupBitmasks = group.bitmasks;
        const groupBitmask = groupBitmasks[eventGenerationId];

        // Determine whether this event affects this group. Pair groups are only
        // affected when the incoming target matches their tracked pair.
        let affected = !!(groupBitmask && (groupBitmask & eventBitflag));
        if (affected && group.pairs && group.pairs.length > 0) {
            const pair = group.pairs[0];
            const pairCtx = pair[$internal];
            const pairRelationTrait = pairCtx.relation[$internal].trait;
            const pairInstance = getTraitInstance(world[$internal].traitInstances, pairRelationTrait);
            const pairGeneration = pairInstance?.generationId;
            const pairBitflag = pairInstance?.bitflag;

            const eventIsPairTrait =
                pairGeneration === eventGenerationId && pairBitflag === eventBitflag;

            if (!eventIsPairTrait) {
                // Regular trait event: pair group unaffected.
                affected = false;
            } else {
                const pairTarget = pairCtx.target;
                if (pairTarget !== '*' && target !== pairTarget) {
                    affected = false;
                }
            }
        }

        if (affected) {
            // Cross-event invalidation:
            // - Remove event invalidates Added/Changed tracking
            // - Add event invalidates Removed/Changed tracking
            if (eventType === 'remove') {
                if (groupType === 'add' || groupType === 'change') return false;
            } else if (eventType === 'add') {
                if (groupType === 'remove' || groupType === 'change') return false;
            }

            // A cancelled opposite pair event must not record a new tracker bit.
            if (pairCanceled) {
                // Fall through to satisfaction check: the group remains unsatisfied.
            } else if (groupType === eventType) {
                // For change events, verify entity still has the trait / pair
                if (eventType === 'change') {
                    if (group.pairs && group.pairs.length > 0) {
                        if (!hasRelationPair(world, entity, group.pairs[0])) return false;
                    } else {
                        const genMasks = entityMasks[eventGenerationId];
                        const entityMask = genMasks ? (genMasks[eid] | 0) : 0;
                        if (!(entityMask & eventBitflag)) return false;
                    }
                }

                // PERF: Cache tracker array reference before mutation
                const groupTrackers = group.trackers;
                let trackerArr = groupTrackers[eventGenerationId];
                if (!trackerArr) {
                    trackerArr = [];
                    groupTrackers[eventGenerationId] = trackerArr;
                }
                trackerArr[eid] = (trackerArr[eid] | 0) | eventBitflag;
            }
        }

cd /app && python3 - <<'PY'
p='packages/core/src/relation/tracking.ts'
s=open(p).read()
# Replace function body from markPairEvent start to return
start=s.index("export function markPairEvent(")
end=s.index("/**\n * Update tracking queries")
old=s[start:end]
new='''export function markPairEvent(
    world: World,
    relationTrait: Trait,
    entity: Entity,
    target: Entity,
    type: 'add' | 'remove' | 'change'
): Map<number, boolean> {
    const ctx = world[$internal];
    const traitId = relationTrait.id;
    const canceledByTracker = new Map<number, boolean>();

    for (const [trackerId, tracker] of ctx.relationTrackers) {
        const eventMap =
            type === 'add'
                ? tracker.added
                : type === 'remove'
                  ? tracker.removed
                  : tracker.changed;

        let byEntity = eventMap.get(traitId);
        if (!byEntity) {
            byEntity = new Map();
            eventMap.set(traitId, byEntity);
        }

        let targets = byEntity.get(entity);
        if (!targets) {
            targets = new Set();
            byEntity.set(entity, targets);
        }

        let canceled = false;

        if (type === 'add') {
            const removedByEntity = tracker.removed.get(traitId);
            const removedTargets = removedByEntity?.get(entity);
            if (removedTargets?.delete(target)) {
                canceled = true;
                if (removedTargets.size === 0) removedByEntity!.delete(entity);
            } else {
                targets.add(target);
            }
        } else if (type === 'remove') {
            const addedByEntity = tracker.added.get(traitId);
            const addedTargets = addedByEntity?.get(entity);
            if (addedTargets?.delete(target)) {
                canceled = true;
                if (addedTargets.size === 0) addedByEntity!.delete(entity);
            } else {
                targets.add(target);
            }
        } else {
            targets.add(target);
        }

        canceledByTracker.set(trackerId, canceled);
    }

    return canceledByTracker;
}

'''
s=s[:start]+new+s[end:]
# update updateQueries signature and checkTracking call
s=s.replace("""export function updateQueriesForRelationPairChange(
    world: World,
    relationTrait: Trait,
    entity: Entity,
    target: Entity,
    eventType: 'add' | 'remove' | 'change',
    canceled = false
) {
    const ctx = world[$internal];
    const instance = getTraitInstance(ctx.traitInstances, relationTrait);
    if (!instance) return;

    const { generationId, bitflag } = instance;

    for (const query of instance.trackingQueries) {
        const match = query.checkTracking(
            world,
            entity,
            eventType,
            generationId,
            bitflag,
            target,
            canceled
        );
        if (match) query.add(entity);
        else query.remove(world, entity);
    }
}""", """export function updateQueriesForRelationPairChange(
    world: World,
    relationTrait: Trait,
    entity: Entity,
    target: Entity,
    eventType: 'add' | 'remove' | 'change',
    canceledByTracker?: Map<number, boolean>
) {
    const ctx = world[$internal];
    const instance = getTraitInstance(ctx.traitInstances, relationTrait);
    if (!instance) return;

    const { generationId, bitflag } = instance;

            world,

            eventType,
            generationId,
            bitflag,
            target,
            canceledByTracker
        );

        else query.remove(world, entity);

}""")

import { createWorld, createAdded, createRemoved, relation } from '/app/packages/core/src/index.ts';
describe('debug cancellation', () => {
 it('x', () => {
  const world = createWorld(); world.init();
  const Added = createAdded();
  const Removed = createRemoved();
  const ChildOf = relation();
  const parent = world.spawn();

import { describe, it, expect } from 'vitest';
import { createWorld, createAdded, createRemoved, relation } from '/app/packages/core/src/index.ts';
describe('debug cancellation', () => {
 it('x', () => {
  const world = createWorld(); world.init();
  const Added = createAdded();
  const Removed = createRemoved();
  const ChildOf = relation();
  const parent = world.spawn();
  const child = world.spawn();
  expect(world.query(Added(ChildOf(parent)))).toHaveLength(0);
  expect(world.query(Removed(ChildOf(parent)))).toHaveLength(0);
  child.add(ChildOf(parent));
  console.log('--- after add');
  child.remove(ChildOf(parent));
  console.log('--- after remove, before query');
  expect(world.query(Added(ChildOf(parent)))).toHaveLength(0);
  expect(world.query(Removed(ChildOf(parent)))).toHaveLength(0);
 });
});

STEP 107 CALL 1 RETURN 0
COMMAND:
cd /app && cat > packages/core/src/relation/tracking.ts <<'EOF'
import { $internal } from '../common';
import type { Entity } from '../entity/types';
import { getEntityId } from '../entity/utils/pack-entity';
import { getTraitInstance } from '../trait/trait-instance';
import type { Trait } from '../trait/types';
import type { World } from '../world';

/** Per-modifier relation pair tracking state. */
export type RelationTracker = {
    /** Initial pairs at factory creation / world reset: relationTraitId -> source -> targets */
    snapshot: Map<number, Map<number, Set<number>>>;
    /** Uncancelled pair additions within observation window */
    added: Map<number, Map<number, Set<number>>>;
    /** Uncancelled pair removals within observation window */
    removed: Map<number, Map<number, Set<number>>>;
    /** Pair changes within observation window */
    changed: Map<number, Map<number, Set<number>>>;
};

export function createRelationTracker(): RelationTracker {
    return {
        snapshot: new Map(),
        added: new Map(),
        removed: new Map(),
        changed: new Map(),
    };
}

/**
 * Record a pair-level event. Opposite add/remove events on the same target cancel.
 * Returns a map from tracking modifier id to whether that modifier's event was cancelled.
 */
export function markPairEvent(
    world: World,
    relationTrait: Trait,
    entity: Entity,
    target: Entity,
    type: 'add' | 'remove' | 'change'
): Map<number, boolean> {
    const ctx = world[$internal];
    const traitId = relationTrait.id;
    const canceledByTracker = new Map<number, boolean>();

    for (const [trackerId, tracker] of ctx.relationTrackers) {
        const eventMap =
            type === 'add'
                ? tracker.added
                : type === 'remove'
                  ? tracker.removed
                  : tracker.changed;

        let byEntity = eventMap.get(traitId);
        if (!byEntity) {
            byEntity = new Map();
            eventMap.set(traitId, byEntity);
        }

        let targets = byEntity.get(entity);
        if (!targets) {
            targets = new Set();
            byEntity.set(entity, targets);
        }

        let canceled = false;

        if (type === 'add') {
            const removedByEntity = tracker.removed.get(traitId);
            const removedTargets = removedByEntity?.get(entity);
            if (removedTargets?.delete(target)) {
                canceled = true;
                if (removedTargets.size === 0) removedByEntity!.delete(entity);
            } else {
                targets.add(target);
            }
        } else if (type === 'remove') {
            const addedByEntity = tracker.added.get(traitId);
            const addedTargets = addedByEntity?.get(entity);
            if (addedTargets?.delete(target)) {
                canceled = true;
                if (addedTargets.size === 0) addedByEntity!.delete(entity);
            } else {
                targets.add(target);
            }
        } else {
            targets.add(target);
        }

        canceledByTracker.set(trackerId, canceled);
    }

    return canceledByTracker;
}

/**
 * Update tracking queries that reference the relation trait after a pair-level event.
 */
export function updateQueriesForRelationPairChange(
    world: World,
    relationTrait: Trait,
    entity: Entity,
    target: Entity,
    eventType: 'add' | 'remove' | 'change',
    canceledByTracker?: Map<number, boolean>
) {
    const ctx = world[$internal];
    const instance = getTraitInstance(ctx.traitInstances, relationTrait);
    if (!instance) return;

    const { generationId, bitflag } = instance;

    for (const query of instance.trackingQueries) {
        const match = query.checkTracking(
            world,
            entity,
            eventType,
            generationId,
            bitflag,
            target,
            canceledByTracker
        );
        if (match) query.add(entity);
        else query.remove(world, entity);
    }
}

/** Get a nested map by relation trait id. */
function getTargetSet(
    map: Map<number, Map<number, Set<number>>>,
    traitId: number,
    entity: Entity
): Set<number> | undefined {
    return map.get(traitId)?.get(entity);
}

/** Check if a specific pair is present in a pair event map. */
export function hasPairEvent(
    world: World,
    trackerId: number,
    relationTrait: Trait,
    entity: Entity,
    target: Entity | '*',
    type: 'add' | 'remove' | 'change'
): boolean {
    const tracker = world[$internal].relationTrackers.get(trackerId);
    if (!tracker) return false;

    const map =
        type === 'add'
            ? tracker.added
            : type === 'remove'
              ? tracker.removed
              : tracker.changed;

    const targets = getTargetSet(map, relationTrait.id, entity);
    if (!targets) return false;
    if (target === '*') return targets.size > 0;
    return targets.has(target);
}

/** Check if a specific pair existed in the initial snapshot. */
export function hasPairSnapshot(
    world: World,
    trackerId: number,
    relationTrait: Trait,
    entity: Entity,
    target: Entity | '*'
): boolean {
    const tracker = world[$internal].relationTrackers.get(trackerId);
    if (!tracker) return false;
    const targets = getTargetSet(tracker.snapshot, relationTrait.id, entity);
    if (!targets) return false;
    if (target === '*') return targets.size > 0;
    return targets.has(target);
}

/** Build the pair snapshot for a tracker from current world state. */
export function populateRelationTrackerSnapshot(world: World, tracker: RelationTracker) {
    const ctx = world[$internal];
    for (const relation of ctx.relations) {
        const relationTrait = relation[$internal].trait;
        const instance = getTraitInstance(ctx.traitInstances, relationTrait);
        if (!instance || !instance.relationTargets) continue;

        const byEntity = new Map<number, Set<number>>();
        tracker.snapshot.set(relationTrait.id, byEntity);

        const targets = instance.relationTargets;
        for (let eid = 0; eid < targets.length; eid++) {
            const relationCtx = relation[$internal];
            let entityTargets: number[] | undefined;
            if (relationCtx.exclusive) {
                const t = (targets as Array<number | undefined>)[eid];
                if (t !== undefined) entityTargets = [t];
            } else {
                entityTargets = (targets as number[][])[eid];
            }
            if (!entityTargets || entityTargets.length === 0) continue;

            // The entity index dense array holds the packed entity for each eid.
            const denseIdx = ctx.entityIndex.sparse[eid];
            const packedEntity = ctx.entityIndex.dense[denseIdx];
            if (packedEntity === undefined || getEntityId(packedEntity) !== eid) continue;

            byEntity.set(packedEntity, new Set(entityTargets));
        }
    }
}
EOF

        }
""", "")
open(p,'w').write(s)

 it('x', () => {

p='packages/core/src/query/utils/check-query-tracking.ts'

old="""            if (!eventIsPairTrait) {
                // Regular trait event: pair group unaffected.
                affected = false;
            } else {
                const pairTarget = pairCtx.target;
                if (pairTarget !== '*' && target !== pairTarget) {
                    affected = false;
                }
            }
        }

new="""            if (!eventIsPairTrait) {
                // Regular trait event: pair group unaffected.
                affected = false;
            } else if (target === undefined) {
                // Trait-level structural event (first add / last remove). We
                // cross-invalidate pair additions/changes on trait removal but
                // never record a tracker here - pair-level events own trackers.
                affected = true;
            } else {
                const pairTarget = pairCtx.target;
                if (pairTarget !== '*' && target !== pairTarget) {
                    affected = false;
                }
            }
        }
"""
assert old in s
s=s.replace(old,new)
# In affected block, handle target-undefined pair groups
old2="""        if (affected) {
            // Cross-event invalidation:
            // - Remove event invalidates Added/Changed tracking
            // - Add event invalidates Removed/Changed tracking
            if (eventType === 'remove') {
                if (groupType === 'add' || groupType === 'change') return false;
            } else if (eventType === 'add') {
                if (groupType === 'remove' || groupType === 'change') return false;
            }

            // A cancelled opposite pair event must not record a new tracker bit.
            const pairCanceled = pairCanceledByTracker?.get(group.id) ?? false;
            if (pairCanceled) {
                // Fall through to satisfaction check: the group remains unsatisfied.
            } else if (groupType === eventType) {"""
new2="""        if (affected) {
            // Cross-event invalidation:
            // - Remove event invalidates Added/Changed tracking
            // - Add event invalidates Removed/Changed tracking
            if (eventType === 'remove') {
                if (groupType === 'add' || groupType === 'change') return false;
            } else if (eventType === 'add') {
                if (groupType === 'remove' || groupType === 'change') return false;
            }

            // A cancelled opposite pair event must not record a new tracker bit.
            // Trait-level structural events (target undefined) also must not record.
            const pairCanceled = pairCanceledByTracker?.get(group.id) ?? false;
            const traitLevelPairEvent = !!group.pairs && group.pairs.length > 0 && target === undefined;
            if (pairCanceled || traitLevelPairEvent) {
                // Fall through to satisfaction check: the group remains unsatisfied.
            } else if (groupType === eventType) {"""

cd /app && cat >> packages/core/tests/pair-tracking-modifiers.test.ts <<'EOF'

describe('pair tracking modifiers edge cases', () => {
    const world = createWorld();
    world.init();

    beforeEach(() => {
        world.reset();
    });

    it('destruction fires pair-level removal for every active pair', () => {
        const Likes = relation();
        const targetA = world.spawn();
        const targetB = world.spawn();
        const entity = world.spawn(Likes(targetA), Likes(targetB));
        const Removed = createRemoved();

        entity.destroy();

        expect(world.query(Removed(Likes(targetA)))).toContain(entity);
        expect(world.query(Removed(Likes(targetB)))).toContain(entity);
    });

    it('wildcard removed matches any removed pair', () => {
        const Likes = relation();
        const targetA = world.spawn();
        const targetB = world.spawn();
        const entityA = world.spawn(Likes(targetA));
        const entityB = world.spawn(Likes(targetB));
        const Removed = createRemoved();

        entityA.remove(Likes(targetA));
        entityB.remove(Likes(targetB));

        const removed = world.query(Removed(Likes('*')));
        expect(removed).toContain(entityA);
        expect(removed).toContain(entityB);
    });

    it('Or composes pair modifiers with regular tracking modifiers', () => {
        const Added = createAdded();
        const Likes = relation();
        const Foo = trait();
        const target = world.spawn();
        const entityA = world.spawn();
        const entityB = world.spawn();

        entityA.add(Likes(target));
        entityB.add(Foo);

        const entities = world.query(Or(Added(Likes(target)), Added(Foo)));
        expect(entities).toContain(entityA);
        expect(entities).toContain(entityB);
    });

    it('pair query created after events is populated from accumulated state', () => {
        const Added = createAdded();
        const Likes = relation();
        const target = world.spawn();
        const entity = world.spawn();
        entity.add(Likes(target));

        // First query drains the event.
        expect(world.query(Added(Likes(target)))).toContain(entity);
        // A second query has no new events.
        expect(world.query(Added(Likes(target)))).toHaveLength(0);

        const targetB = world.spawn();
        const entityB = world.spawn();
        entityB.add(Likes(targetB));
        expect(world.query(Added(Likes(targetB)))).toContain(entityB);
    });
});
EOF
cd packages/core && pnpm exec vitest run tests/pair-tracking-modifiers.test.ts 2>&1 | tail -60

STEP 118 CALL 1 RETURN 0
COMMAND:
cd /app && cat >> packages/core/tests/pair-tracking-modifiers.test.ts <<'EOF'

describe('pair tracking modifiers query result with Or', () => {
    const world = createWorld();
    world.init();

    beforeEach(() => {
        world.reset();
    });

    it('readEach resolves per-target data through Or', () => {
        const Added = createAdded();
        const Contains = relation({ store: { amount: 0 } });
        const targetA = world.spawn();
        const targetB = world.spawn();
        const entityA = world.spawn();
        const entityB = world.spawn();
        entityA.add(Contains(targetA, { amount: 1 }));
        entityB.add(Contains(targetB, { amount: 2 }));

        const seen = new Map<number, number>();
        world
            .query(Or(Added(Contains(targetA)), Added(Contains(targetB))))
            .readEach(([contains], entity) => {
                seen.set(entity.id(), (contains as { amount: number }).amount);
            });

        expect(seen.get(entityA.id())).toBe(1);
        expect(seen.get(entityB.id())).toBe(2);
    });
});
EOF

new="""        const seen = new Map<number, number>();
        world
            .query(Or(Added(Contains(targetA)), Added(Contains(targetB))))
            .readEach(([containsA, containsB], entity) => {
                const amount =
                    (containsA as { amount?: number } | undefined)?.amount ??
                    (containsB as { amount?: number } | undefined)?.amount;
                seen.set(entity.id(), amount);
            });

        expect(seen.get(entityA.id())).toBe(1);
        expect(seen.get(entityB.id())).toBe(2);"""
assert old in s
s=s.replace(old,new)
open(p,'w').write(s)
print('test updated')

cd /app && cat >> packages/core/tests/pair-tracking-modifiers.test.ts <<'EOF'

describe('pair tracking modifiers additional', () => {
    const world = createWorld();
    world.init();

    beforeEach(() => {
        world.reset();
    });

    it('a single modifier can combine a pair and a regular trait with AND', () => {
        const Added = createAdded();
        const Likes = relation();
        const Foo = trait();
        const target = world.spawn();
        const withFoo = world.spawn(Foo);
        const withoutFoo = world.spawn();

        withFoo.add(Likes(target));
        withoutFoo.add(Likes(target));

        const entities = world.query(Added(Likes(target), Foo));
        expect(entities).toContain(withFoo);
        expect(entities).not.toContain(withoutFoo);
    });

    it('destroying a target fires pair-level removal on its sources', () => {
        const Likes = relation();
        const target = world.spawn();
        const source = world.spawn(Likes(target));
        const Removed = createRemoved();

        target.destroy();

        const removed = world.query(Removed(Likes(target)));
        expect(removed).toContain(source);
    });
});
EOF

describe('pair tracking modifiers hash with Or', () => {
    const world = createWorld();
    world.init();

    beforeEach(() => {
        world.reset();
    });

    it('Or pair queries with different targets have distinct hashes', () => {
        const Added = createAdded();
        const Likes = relation();
        const parentA = world.spawn();
        const parentB = world.spawn();

        world.query(Or(Added(Likes(parentA)), Added(Likes(parentA))));
        const sizeA = world[$internal].queriesHashMap.size;

        world.query(Or(Added(Likes(parentA)), Added(Likes(parentB))));
        const sizeB = world[$internal].queriesHashMap.size;

        expect(sizeB).toBeGreaterThan(sizeA);
    });
});
EOF

## Relation events

Relations emit events per **pair**. This makes it easy to know exactly which target was involved.

---readme---
// Track when any entity removes the ChildOf relation
const orphaned = world.query(Removed(ChildOf))

// Track when relation data changes for any target
const updated = world.query(Changed(ChildOf))
```

> Tracking modifiers do not accept pairs directly such as `Changed(ChildOf(parent))`. Instead, pass the base relation to the modifier and add the pair as a separate query parameter to filter by target.

```js
const parent = world.spawn()

// Filter changed entities by a specific target
const changedChildren = world.query(Changed(ChildOf), ChildOf(parent))
```

## Tracking modifiers

Track structural and data changes. Each tracking modifier must be created as a unique instance.

```typescript
import { createAdded, createRemoved, createChanged } from 'koota'

// Create unique instances (typically at module scope)
const Added = createAdded()
const Removed = createRemoved()
const Changed = createChanged()
```

**Added** - Entities that added a trait since last query:

```typescript
const newPositions = world.query(Added(Position))

// Track relation additions
const newChildren = world.query(Added(ChildOf))
```

**Removed** - Entities that removed a trait since last query (includes destroyed entities):

```typescript
const stoppedEntities = world.query(Removed(Velocity))

// Track orphaned entities
const orphaned = world.query(Removed(ChildOf))
```

**Changed** - Entities whose trait data changed since last query:

```typescript
const movedEntities = world.query(Changed(Position))

// Track relation data changes
const updatedChildren = world.query(Changed(ChildOf))
```

**Logical AND (default):**

When multiple traits are passed to a tracking modifier, it uses logical AND. Only entities where **all** specified traits match the condition are returned:

```typescript
// Entities where BOTH Position AND Velocity were added
const fullyAdded = world.query(Added(Position, Velocity))

// Entities where BOTH Position AND Velocity were removed
const fullyRemoved = world.query(Removed(Position, Velocity))

// Entities where BOTH Position AND Velocity have changed
const fullyUpdated = world.query(Changed(Position, Velocity))
---relations---
# Relations

Relations build graphs between entities. Use for hierarchies, inventories, targeting, neighbor networks, and any entity-to-entity connection.

## Contents

- [Core Concepts](#core-concepts)
- [Basic Syntax](#basic-syntax)
- [Building Graphs](#building-graphs) - Hierarchies, inventories, targeting, neighbors
- [Querying Relations](#querying-relations) - Specific targets, wildcards, combined queries
- [Traversing Graphs](#traversing-graphs) - Recursive traversal, building trees, finding ancestors
- [Ordered Relations](#ordered-relations) - Maintaining order (experimental)
- [Removing Relations](#removing-relations)
- [Relation Options](#relation-options)
- [React Hooks](#react-hooks)
- [Anti-Patterns](#anti-patterns) - Common mistakes to avoid

## Core Concepts

A relation connects a **source** entity to a **target** entity. The source owns the relation.

```
┌─────────┐  ChildOf(Parent)  ┌─────────┐
│  Child  │ ─────────────────▶│ Parent  │
│ (source)│                   │ (target)│
└─────────┘                   └─────────┘
```

The child expresses the relationship, not the parent. This enables efficient batch queries for all children of a parent.

## Basic Syntax

```typescript
import { relation } from 'koota'

// Basic relation (no data)
const ChildOf = relation()

// Relation with data
const Contains = relation({ store: { amount: 0 } })

// Auto cleanup when target destroyed
const ChildOf = relation({ autoDestroy: 'orphan' })

// Only one target allowed per entity
const Targeting = relation({ exclusive: true })
```

## Building Graphs

### Hierarchies (parent-child)

```typescript
const ChildOf = relation({ autoDestroy: 'orphan' })

const parent = world.spawn()
const child = world.spawn(ChildOf(parent))
const grandchild = world.spawn(ChildOf(child))

// Destroying parent destroys entire subtree
parent.destroy() // child and grandchild also destroyed
```

### Inventories (contains)

```typescript
const Contains = relation({ store: { amount: 0 } })

const inventory = world.spawn()
const gold = world.spawn()
const sword = world.spawn()

inventory.add(Contains(gold, { amount: 100 }))
inventory.add(Contains(sword, { amount: 1 }))

// Update amount
inventory.set(Contains(gold), { amount: 50 })

// Read amount
const data = inventory.get(Contains(gold)) // { amount: 50 }
```

### Targeting/Following

```typescript
const Targeting = relation({ exclusive: true })

const enemy = world.spawn()
const player = world.spawn()
const otherPlayer = world.spawn()

enemy.add(Targeting(player))
enemy.add(Targeting(otherPlayer)) // Replaces previous target

enemy.has(Targeting(player)) // false
enemy.has(Targeting(otherPlayer)) // true
```

### Neighbor Networks

```typescript
const NeighborOf = relation()

// Build bidirectional connections
entityA.add(NeighborOf(entityB))
entityB.add(NeighborOf(entityA))
```

## Querying Relations

### Query children of specific parent

```typescript
const children = world.query(ChildOf(parent))

for (const child of children) {
  // Process each child
}
```

### Query all entities with any relation (wildcard)

```typescript
// All entities that are children of something
const allChildren = world.query(ChildOf('*'))

// All entities that contain something
const allContainers = world.query(Contains('*'))
```

### Get targets from an entity

```typescript
// Get all targets
const items = entity.targetsFor(Contains) // Entity[]

// Get first target
const target = entity.targetFor(Targeting) // Entity | undefined
```

### Combined queries

```typescript
// Enemies targeting the player
const threats = world.query(IsEnemy, Targeting(player))

// Children of parent that also have Position
const positionedChildren = world.query(ChildOf(parent), Position)
```

## Traversing Graphs

### Recursive traversal

```typescript
function traverseFromNode(world: World, node: Entity, depth = 0) {
  console.log('  '.repeat(depth) + `Node ${node.id()}`)

  const children = world.query(ChildOf(node))
  for (const child of children) {

import type { Schema } from './types';

function createSoASetFunction(schema: Schema) {
    const keys = Object.keys(schema);

    // Generate a hardcoded set function based on the schema keys
    const setFunctionBody = keys
        .map((key) => `if ('${key}' in value) store.${key}[index] = value.${key};`)
        .join('\n    ');

    // Use new Function to create a set function with hardcoded keys
    const set = new Function(
        'index',
        'store',
        'value',
        `
		${setFunctionBody}
	  `
    );

    return set;
}

function createSoAFastSetFunction(schema: Schema) {
    const keys = Object.keys(schema);

    // Generate a hardcoded set function based on the schema keys
    const setFunctionBody = keys.map((key) => `store.${key}[index] = value.${key};`).join('\n    ');

    // Use new Function to create a set function with hardcoded keys
    const set = new Function(
        'index',
        'store',
        'value',
        `
		${setFunctionBody}
	  `
    );

    return set;
}

// Return true if any trait value were changed.
function createSoAFastSetChangeFunction(schema: Schema) {
    const keys = Object.keys(schema);

    // Generate a hardcoded set function based on the schema keys
    const setFunctionBody = keys
        .map(
            (key) =>
                `if (store.${key}[index] !== value.${key}) {
            store.${key}[index] = value.${key};
            changed = true;
        }`
        )
        .join('\n    ');

    // Use new Function to create a set function with hardcoded keys
    const set = new Function(
        'index',
        'store',
        'value',
        `
        let changed = false;
        ${setFunctionBody}
        return changed;
        `
    );

    return set;
}

function createSoAGetFunction(schema: Schema) {
    const keys = Object.keys(schema);

    // Create an object literal with all keys assigned from the store
    const objectLiteral = `{ ${keys.map((key) => `${key}: store.${key}[index]`).join(', ')} }`;

    // Use new Function to create a get function that returns the pre-populated object
    const get = new Function(
        'index',
        'store',
        `
        return ${objectLiteral};
        `
    );

    return get;
}

function createAoSSetFunction(_schema: Schema) {
    return (index: number, store: any, value: any) => {
        store[index] = value;
    };
}

function createAoSFastSetChangeFunction(_schema: Schema) {
    return (index: number, store: any, value: any) => {
        let changed = false;
        if (value !== store[index]) {
            store[index] = value;
            changed = true;
        }
        return changed;
    };
}

function createAoSGetFunction(_schema: Schema) {
    return (index: number, store: any) => store[index];
}

const noop = () => {};
const createTagNoop = () => noop;

export const createSetFunction = {
    soa: createSoASetFunction,
    aos: createAoSSetFunction,
    tag: createTagNoop,
};

export const createFastSetFunction = {
    soa: createSoAFastSetFunction,
    aos: createAoSSetFunction,
    tag: createTagNoop,
};

export const createFastSetChangeFunction = {
    soa: createSoAFastSetChangeFunction,
    aos: createAoSFastSetChangeFunction,
    tag: createTagNoop,
};

export const createGetFunction = {
    soa: createSoAGetFunction,
    aos: createAoSGetFunction,
    tag: createTagNoop,
};

cd /app && cat >> packages/core/tests/pair-tracking-modifiers.test.ts <<'EOF'

describe('pair tracking modifiers across reset', () => {
    const world = createWorld();
    world.init();

    it('pair factories keep working after reset', () => {
        const Added = createAdded();
        const Likes = relation();
        const parent = world.spawn();
        const child = world.spawn();
        child.add(Likes(parent));

        world.reset();

        const parent2 = world.spawn();
        const child2 = world.spawn();
        child2.add(Likes(parent2));

        const entities = world.query(Added(Likes(parent2)));
        expect(entities).toContain(child2);
    });
});
EOF

STEP 158 CALL 1 RETURN 0
COMMAND:
cd /app && cat > /tmp/check_extra.mts <<'EOF'
import { createWorld, createAdded, createRemoved, createChanged, relation, Or } from '/app/packages/core/src/index.ts';
const world = createWorld(); world.init();
const Likes = relation({ store: { amount: 0 } });
const a = world.spawn(); const b = world.spawn();
const e1 = world.spawn(Likes(a)); const e2 = world.spawn(Likes(b));
const Removed = createRemoved(); const Changed = createChanged();
e1.remove(Likes(a));
e2.remove(Likes(b));
const qr = world.query(Or(Removed(Likes(a)), Removed(Likes(b))));
console.log('removed or', qr.includes(e1), qr.includes(e2));
const e3 = world.spawn(Likes(a));
e3.set(Likes(a), { amount: 5 });
const qc = world.query(Or(Changed(Likes(a)), Changed(Likes(b))));
console.log('changed or', qc.includes(e3), qc.length);
EOF
pnpm exec tsx /tmp/check_extra.mts

import { createAdded, createWorld, relation } from '/app/packages/core/src/index.ts';
const Added = createAdded(); // before world
const world = createWorld();
world.init();
const Likes = relation();
const p = world.spawn();
const e = world.spawn();

const q = world.query(Added(Likes(p)));

EOF
